Let's design local score function normalized:
```
def score_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    S=np.sum(a)
    if S<=0: return inf
    a=a/S
    c=fft_convolve(a,a)
    return 2*n*np.max(c)
```

So we need cross-corr. Implementation:
```
def conv(a,b,nfft): ...
def grad_from_weights(p,w,nfft):
    revw = w[::-1]
    c=conv(p, revw, nfft)  # length L
    T = c[L-1:-1:-1]  # first n? Since c length L, c[::-1][:n]
    return 2*T
```
Note revw length L. C length L. `c[::-1]` length L. T[:n]? We need T_i for i=0..n-1 = C[L-1-i]. So T = C[L-1 : L-1-n : -1]. If C length L=2n-1, this slice length n. Good.

Self convolution c = conv(p,p). We can compute Fp once. For cross-corr, need FFT of revw. We can also compute Fw_rev via `np.fft.rfft(revw, nfft)`. So one forward p, one forward revw, inverse c (Fp^2), inverse T (Fp*Fw_rev).

In top-k/soft weights, if weights are symmetric w=w_rev, cross-corr T_i =? Could maybe use correlation but no matter.

Need compute initial sequences:
```
def central_binomial_seq(n):
   a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
   return a/sum(a)
```
For general α:
```
def power_seq(n, alpha):
   a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(i+alpha-1)/i
   # For large alpha may overflow? n<=1024, alpha<=2, ratio ~1; fine. For alpha<1 decays.
   a=np.clip(a,1e-300,None); return a/sum(a)
```
For alpha=0? undefined; uniform separate.

Detailed optimizer:
```
def run_optimizer(n, init_p, refs, time_limit, rng, name):
    p=positive_norm(init_p)
    theta=np.log(p)
    m,v zeros; step=0
    best_p=p; best_score=score_p(p); best_theta=theta
    cur_p=p; cur_score=best_score
    t0=now
    patience=1000 (steps since improvement in checks)
    last_check=0
    stage_index=0
    while time<limit:
       frac=(time-t0)/time_limit
       # choose topk,tau,mix
       c, w, g = compute_weights_grad(p,...)
       # Adam
       step+=1
       lr=0.08*(1-0.5*frac)
       m=...; v=...
       theta = theta - lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v)+1e-8)
       theta -= np.max(theta)  # stable
       # maybe every k iterations do p=softmax(theta); check
       if step % check_interval ==0:
          p=softmax(theta); sc=score_p(p)
          if sc < cur_score:
             cur_score=sc; p_cur=p
             if sc < best_score:
                best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy(); best_theta=theta.copy(); no_improve=0
             else: no_improve += check_interval
          else:
             no_improve += check_interval
          if no_improve > patience:
             # restart strategy
             choice=rng.random()
             if choice<0.25 or last restart failed:
                # random perturbation of best
                scale=rng.choice([0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4])
                theta = best_theta + scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
                p=softmax(theta)
             elif choice<0.5 and refs:
                ref = refs[rng.integers(len(refs))].copy()
                t=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)
                p = (1-t)*best_p + t*ref
                p=pos_norm(p); theta=np.log(p)
             else:
                # mix with reversed/ref? reversed same score; use random structured
             m,v reset; no_improve=0
             # line search maybe evaluate new p; if score worse by >0.1%, abort? We accept to escape.
```

Let's define `compute_gradient(p, topk, tau_rel, mix, nfft)`:
```
L=2*n-1
c=self_conv(p)  # returns L
maxc=c.max()
if tau_rel>0:
   tau=max(tau_rel*maxc, 1e-12)
   z=(c-maxc)/tau
   w_soft=np.exp(z - z.max()); w_soft/=w_soft.sum()
else w_soft=zeros
if topk>0:
   if topk>=L: w=topk uniform
   else:
      if topk==1: idx=[argmax]
      else: idx=c.argpartition(-topk)[-topk:]
      w_topk=zeros; w_topk[idx]=1/topk
      if mix==0: w=w_topk
      else: w=mix*w_topk+(1-mix)*w_soft; w/=w.sum()
   sym = (w+w[::-1])*0.5
   g=grad_from_w(p, w)
else soft only
return c,g,maxc
```

Fast self_conv:
```
def self_conv(p,nfft):
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,nfft)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```
Since p sum1, central etc. Use `nfft` cache in dict? Top-level global `NFFT=[]`? Function no closures. Could use a dict passed? Simpler compute inside with global variable? We can have top-level `NFFT_CACHE = {}`. Function uses global dict. Allowed top-level.

```
def get_nfft(n):
    if n not in NFFT_CACHE:
       L=2*n-1
       size=1
       while size < L: size <<=1
       NFFT_CACHE[n]=size
    return NFFT_CACHE[n]
```
For cross-corr, using same size.

`grad_from_weight(p,w,n)`:

```
But `c[::-1][:n]` indexing reversed full length. For L=5,n=3, c[::-1][:3] gives [c4,c3,c2]. Need T_i=c[L-1-i] for i=0..2 => [c4,c3,c2]. Correct. Good.

Could combine self_conv and grad_from_weight to avoid recomputing Fp? In `compute_grad`, we can compute Fp once, self c and grad c with Fwr. Let's implement:
```
def compute_grad(p, n, topk, tau_rel, mix, sym_weights, nfft=None):
    if nfft is None: nfft=get_nfft(n)
    L=2*n-1
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,nfft)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    # weights
    maxc=c.max()
    if topk > 0:
       ... w_topk
    if tau_rel>0:
       tau=max(tau_rel*maxc,1e-15)
       z=(c-maxc)/tau
       ws=np.exp(z-z.max()); ws/=ws.sum()
    else ws=None
    if topk>0 and ws is not None and 0<mix<1: w=mix*w_topk+(1-mix)*ws
    elif topk>0: w=w_topk
    else: w=ws
    if sym_weights: w=0.5*(w+w[::-1])
    Fwr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],nfft)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fwr,nfft)[:L]
    T=conv[::-1][:n]
    g=2.0*T
    return c,g,maxc
```
For topk with mix, w_topk normalized. If topk=1 and sym true, weight on two coefficients 0.5. Good.

Need handle if topk >= L: set uniform? But L large. topk schedule small. If topk> L, w_topk uniform over all (zero gradient). We'll cap topk < L//2 maybe.

`try_multiplicative_step`:
```
def multi_step(p,n,g,c,scales):
   scale = 1/(np.max(np.abs(g))+1e-12)
   base = p.copy(); score=score_p(p)
   best=base
   for s in scales:
       theta = np.log(p) - s*scale*g? Wait g is derivative wrt p. In log update, p_i *= exp(-η * g_i) maybe. Need subtract constant not needed. Normalize.
       p2 = p * np.exp(-h * g) where h=s*scale? If g ~1/n, scale*n? Let's set h = s / (np.linalg.norm(g,1)?) Better: choose h such that max log change ~s. Use g_norm = max(abs(g)); h=s/g_norm. Then p2=p*np.exp(-h*g) ; normalize. But subtracting mean not needed. Evaluate.
```
This is a line search in natural gradient direction for current weights. It may be expensive (multiple scores). Use when stuck only.

   new_h = H@new_lambda

Let's design `active_gradient(p, c, K, fw_iters)`:
```
if K>=len(c): # top all, gradient zero? Use uniform? But K schedule small.
idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K<len(c) else c full
# sort maybe not needed
H=[]
for k in idx:
   T=zeros(n); lo=...; T[lo:hi+1]=p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   d=2*T
   inner=p.dot(d)
   h=p*(d-inner)
   H.append(h)
H=np.array(H)
# If all h zero, return zeros
# Frank-Wolfe min norm
lam=np.full(K,1/K)
h=H@lam
norm_h=np.linalg.norm(h)
best_lam=lam; best_h=h
for t in range(fw_iters):
   grad=H.dot(h)  # directional derivative of norm^2: 2 H h, constant ignored
   j=int(np.argmin(grad))
   gamma=2.0/(t+2)
   lam_new=(1-gamma)*lam + gamma
   lam_new[jj]=...; or create e.
   h_new=H@lam_new
   nnew=np.linalg.norm(h_new)
   if nnew < norm_h - 1e-12:
       lam=lam_new; h=h_new; norm_h=nnew
return h
```

Implementation main loop with active gradient:
```
def optimize_active(n, init_p, time_limit, rng, refs, name):
    p = positive_norm(init_p)
    theta = np.log(p)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); bias1=1; bias2=1; step=0
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=2*n*self_conv(p).max()/sum^2? (p norm)
    best_theta=theta.copy()
    no_improve=0
    t0=...
    prev_score=best_score
    while time < limit:
       p=softmax(theta)
       c=self_conv(p)
       score=2*n*c.max()
       if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
       frac=...
       K = schedule(...)
       if K<=1:
           idx=[argmax]
       else: top idx
       h=active_gradient(p,c,K,rng? )
       if h is None or norm==0: h=tangent gradient? use argmax fallback.
       step+=1
       m=... v=...
       lr=...
       theta -= lr*mhat/(sqrt(v)+eps)
       theta -= np.max(theta)
       if no_improve > patience:
           # line search / restart
           achieved=False
           # Try multiplicative line search from best with current h? Use best p and its c?
           # Simpler: random perturb/mix
           ... reset theta,m,v; no_improve=0
           achieved if new score < best*(1+0.02) or better
```

def active_k(frac,n):
    if frac<0.15: return max(8, int(0.12*n))
    elif frac<0.4: return max(4, int(0.06*n))
    elif frac<0.7: return max(4, int(0.02*n))

    else: return 1
```

def active_gradient(p, c, K, rng, sym_pairs=False):
   L=len(c)
   K=min(K,L)
   if K==1:
      idx=[int(np.argmax(c))]
   else:
      part=np.argpartition(c, -K)
      idx=part[-K:].copy()
      # random tweak: if rng.random()<0.15 and K<L: choose one random from part? The part array includes unsorted; not easy top 2K. Instead select top 2K partition then sample.
      if rng is not None and K < L//2 and rng.random() < 0.1:
         top2K=np.argpartition(c, -2*K)[-2*K:]
         idx[int(rng.integers(K))] = int(rng.choice(top2K))
      idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
   H=np.empty((K,n))
   if K==1:
      k=idx[0]; T=zeros; assign; d=2T; inner=dot; H[0]=p*(d-inner)
   else:
      for t,k in enumerate(idx): ...
   # FW
   lam=np.full(K,1.0/K)
   h=H@lam
   norm_h=np.dot(h,h)**0.5
   best_h=h; best_lam=lam
   # If K>1:
   for it in range(fw_iters):
       # directional derivative constant
       Hv=H.dot(h)
       j=int(np.argmin(Hv))
       gamma=2.0/(it+2)
       # create new lam efficiently
       lam_new=(1-gamma)*lam
       lam_new[j] += gamma
       h_new=H.dot(lam_new)
       norm_new=np.dot(h_new,h_new)**0.5
       if norm_new < norm_h - 1e-15:
           lam=lam_new; h=h_new; norm_h=norm_new
   if norm_h < 1e-14:
       # fallback: use first (largest peak)
       return H[0].copy()
   return h
```

Now, initial positive normalization:
```
def positive_normalize(arr):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
    S=arr.sum()
    if S<=0:
       return np.ones(len(arr))/len(arr)
    arr=arr/S
    # ensure positive? For active gradient, zero okay but floors.
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-12)
    arr /= arr.sum()
    return arr
```

Let's detail `optimize_active`:
```
def optimize_active(n, init_seq, refs, time_limit, rng, verbose=True):
    if time_limit <= 0: return None, inf
    p = positive_normalize(init_seq)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-14)
    p /= p.sum()
    theta = np.log(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    c = self_convolution(p, n)
    best_score = 2.0*n*c.max()
    theta_best = theta.copy()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); bias1=1.0; bias2=1.0; step=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_last_best=t0; last_print=t0
    patience_s=20.0
    printed=False
    while time.time? use monoton.
       # compute next p? We can start by using current p and theta; after update, loop.
       # We need current score; p and c maybe from previous. We'll maintain p,c,score for current theta.
       if step==0: p=... c=...
       else: p=softmax(theta); c=self_convolution(p,n)
       score=2*n*max(c)
       if score < best_score - 1e-15:
           best_score=score; theta_best=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); t_last_best=time.monotonic()
       # print if >60
       # if time limit almost done break? 
       frac=(now-t0)/time_limit
       if frac>=1: break
       K=schedule
       h=active_gradient(p,c,K,rng)
       # direction search if stuck?
       if now - t_last_best > patience_s:
           # try line search along h from current/best
           found=False
           # Use normalized h
           hn=np.linalg.norm(h)
           if hn>1e-14:
              dirn=h/hn
              for s in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]:
                  th_try=theta - s*dirn
                  p_try=softmax_probs(th_try)
                  c_try=self_convolution(p_try,n)
                  sc_try=2*n*c_try.max()
                  if sc_try < best_score:
                      theta=th_try; p=p_try; ...; update best?; return? continue optimize with improved.
                      found=True; break
           if not found:
               # restart
               restart_accepted=False
               for attempt in range(3):
                  choice=rng.random()
                  if choice<0.4:
                     scale=rng.choice([0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4])
                     th_try=theta_best + scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
                  elif choice<0.7 and len(refs)>0:
                     ref=refs[int(rng.integers(len(refs)))].copy()
                     mix=rng.uniform(0.1,0.8)
                     p_try=(1-mix)*best_p + mix*ref
                     p_try=positive_normalize(p_try)
                     th_try=np.log(p_try)
                  else:
                     p_try=p + 0.05*(rng.random(n)-0.5)? better log.
                  sc_try,...
                  if sc_try < best_score*(1.01) or rng.random()<0.1:
                      theta=th_try; p=positive?; reset m,v,bias; t_last_best=now; break
               if not restart: keep current, set t_last_best=now (wait more)
       # Adam update
       step+=1
       lr=0.2*(1.0-0.75*frac)+0.005
       m=0.9*m+0.1*h; v=0.999*v+0.001*h*h; bias1*=0.9; bias2*=0.999
       m_hat=m/(1-bias1); v_hat=v/(1-bias2)
       theta = theta - lr*m_hat/(np.sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8)
       # normalize theta for stability
       theta -= np.max(theta)
       # Optionally floor theta: if theta.min() < -40: theta=np.maximum(theta,-40)
       if theta.min() < -40: theta=np.maximum(theta,-40)
```

```
def score_probs(p,n,nfft=None):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   S=p.sum()
   if S<=0: return inf
   p=p/S
   c=self_convolution(p,n,nfft)
   return 2*n*np.max(c)
```

Let's refine `positive_normalize` to preserve sum and positive:
```
def positive_normalize(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1e12)
    if x.sum()<1e-12: x=np.ones(x.shape)
    x=x/x.sum()
    floor=1e-12
    x = np.maximum(x, floor)
    x = x / x.sum()
    return x
```
If x has length n and some zeros, floor adds 1e-12 each. For n=1024 total floor 1e-9, negligible.

```
def active_direction(...):
    L=len(c); K=min(K,L)
    if K==1:
       idxs=[int(np.argmax(c))]
    else:
       part=np.argpartition(c, -K); idxs=part[-K:]
       if random: ...
       idxs=idxs[np.argsort(c[idxs])[::-1]]
    D=np.empty((K,n)); H=np.empty((K,n))
    for t,k in enumerate(idxs):
       T=np.zeros(n)
       lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(k,n-1)
       if hi>=lo:
          T[lo:hi+1] = p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
       drow=2.0*T
       D[t]=drow
       mrow=np.dot(p,drow)
       H[t]=p*(drow-mrow)
    if K==1: lam=np.array([1.0])
    else:
       lam=np.full(K,1.0/K)
       h=H@lam
       # FW minimize ||h||
       for it in range(fw_iters):
          hv=H.dot(h)
          j=int(np.argmin(hv))
          gamma=2.0/(it+2)
          new_lam=(1-gamma)*lam; new_lam[j]+=gamma
          new_h=H@new_lam
          if np.dot(new_h,new_h) < np.dot(h,h):
              h=new_h; lam=new_lam
       if np.dot(h,h) < 1e-30*max(1.0,np.sum(H*H)/K?): # fallback uniform
           lam=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    dir = D@lam
    center = np.dot(p, dir)
    dir = dir - center
    # Optionally if dir norm too small, use H@lam? or top1 D-m
    if np.dot(dir,dir) < 1e-30:
       dir = H@lam
       # if still zero, derive top1
       if ...: k=idxs[0]; construct drow; dir=drow - dot(p,drow)
    return dir
```

```
def active_direction(p, c, K, tau_rel, rng=None):
    L=c.size; K=min(K,L)
    if K<=1:
       k=int(np.argmax(c)); lam=1.0; # build dir directly and return centered
    else:
       top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       tv=c[top]
       # Sort top by c desc to maybe put top first (not necessary for dir)
       order=np.argsort(tv)[::-1]
       top=top[order]; tv=tv[order]
       if rng is not None and rng.random()<0.08 and K < L//2:
           # replace one with random from top 2K? partition again? Maybe use np.random from top? Simpler: pick one idx from top and replace with random int in range(0,L)
           j=int(rng.integers(K))
           top[j]=int(rng.integers(L))
           tv[j]=c[top[j]]
       maxc=tv[0] if served? use c.max maybe. Use maxc=c.max()
       if tau_rel <=0: lam = 1/K all
       else:
          tau=max(tau_rel*maxc, 1e-300)
          z=(tv-maxc)/tau
          z-=z.max()
          lam=np.exp(z); s=lam.sum(); lam=lam/s
    dir=np.zeros(n)
    if K==1:
       k=top? ; add with coeff1
    else:
       for t,k in enumerate(top):
          w=lam[t]
          if w==0: continue
          lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(k,n-1)
          dir[lo:hi+1] += (2.0*w) * p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
    center=np.dot(p,dir)
    dir -= center
    return dir
```
If random replacement creates duplicate, weights sum still1; okay. If many duplicate, dir may double? Duplicated k rows with separate weights, equivalent weight sum; fine.

Let's implement `self_convolution` with zero negative:
```
def self_convolution(a, n, nfft=None):
    if nfft is None: nfft=get_nfft(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```

```
def score_probs(seq, n, nfft=None):
    a=np.asarray(seq,float)
    S=a.sum()
    if not finite or S<=0: return inf
    a=a/S
    c=self_convolution(a,n,nfft)
    return float(2*n*max(c))
```

```

Line search:
```
def try_line_search(theta, dir, n, best_score):
    norm=np.linalg.norm(dir)
    if norm<1e-14: return None
    u=dir/norm
    for step in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
       th=theta - step*u
       sc,p=score_of_theta(th,n)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           return th,p,sc
    return None
```
Maybe also try step `0.03` etc. If best_score small, finding improvement <1e-12 maybe hard. Use `sc < best_score` okay.

Restart strategies:
```
def propose_restart(best_p, best_theta, refs, n, rng, best_score):
    attempts=4
    for _ in range(attempts):
       r=rng.random()
       if r<0.4:
          scale=float(rng.choice([0.03,0.07,0.15,0.3]))
          th=best_theta + scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
       elif r<0.75 and refs:
          ref=refs[rng.integers(len(refs))].copy()
          mix=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.6))
          p=(1-mix)*best_p + mix*ref
          p=positive_normalize(p)
          th=np.log(p); th-=th.max()
       else:
          # scale best_p by random multiplicative noise
          p=best_p * np.exp(0.2*rng.standard_normal(n))
          p=positive_normalize(p)
          th=np.log(p); th-=th.max()
       sc,p=score_of_theta(th,n)
       if sc < best_score or sc < best_score*(1.005) and rng.random()<0.5? Need avoid too accepting.
       accept if sc < best_score*(1.01) maybe. But if all worse, may accept worst to escape. Use: if sc < best_score or (attempts==1 and sc < best_score*1.05) or rng.random()<0.1: accept.
    return th,p,sc,accepted
```
In loop, if line search found improvement, accept and reset. If restart accepted, set theta, p? We store theta. Reset m/v/bias. Set `t_last_best=now` even if worse to avoid immediate. But if sc significantly worse, maybe not optimize? It might slowly recover. Accept with probability. Use only if sc < best_score*1.03 or random0.2.

`interpolate_sequence`: If provider length maybe >0. Use `np.interp`. If len==1, constant.
```
def interpolate_sequence(seq, n):
   seq=np.asarray(seq,float); seq=np.clip(seq,0,None)
   if seq.ndim!=1: seq=seq.ravel()
   m=seq.size
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   if m==n: p=seq.copy()
   else:
      xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
      p=np.interp(xn,xo,seq)
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   if p.sum()<=0: p=np.ones(n)
   return positive_normalize(p)
```

   d=np.zeros(n)

   return d

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)

by direct positive-space optimization in log-probability coordinates.

The main optimizer uses a top-k active-set peak model.  From the current
sequence, it selects the largest convolution coefficients, computes the
corresponding unprojected derivatives, and forms a weighted derivative
direction.  It then performs Adam steps in log-space, with time-based
annealing of the active-set size, periodic line search, and reference-mixture
restarts.  Several initial families are generated: the supplied sequence,
central-binomial power-law trials, mildly tilted trials, and simple smooth
shapes.  Multiple lengths are explored, and the final best candidate is
verified with the provided evaluate_sequence.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


NFFT_CACHE = {}


def get_nfft(n):
    """Return the first power of two at least 2n-1."""
    if n in NFFT_CACHE:
        return NFFT_CACHE[n]
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < L:
        size <<= 1
    NFFT_CACHE[n] = size
    return size


def self_convolution(a, n, nfft=None):
    """Linear convolution a*a normalized to start at index 0."""
    if nfft is None:
        nfft = get_nfft(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfft)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def normalize_probs(p, floor=1e-12):
    """Normalize a nonnegative array and impose a small positive floor."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p.size)
    p = p / s
    if floor and floor > 0.0:
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        p = p / float(np.sum(p))
    return p


def positive_normalize(x):
    """Convenience wrapper for normalize_probs with a robust floor."""
    return normalize_probs(x, floor=1e-12)


def softmax_probs(theta):
    """Stable softmax of a vector of logits."""
    z = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / z.size
    return e / s


def score_probs(p, n, nfft=None):
    """Local score for a normalized (or unnormalized) probability vector."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size != n:
        p = normalize_probs(p)
        if p.size != n:
            return np.inf
    p = normalize_probs(p, floor=1e-300)
    c = self_convolution(p, n, nfft)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))


def score_of_theta(theta, n, nfft=None):
    """Score and probability vector corresponding to logits theta."""
    p = softmax_probs(theta)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        p = p / s
    c = self_convolution(p, n, nfft)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))), p


def central_binomial_seq(n):
    """Truncated coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), normalized later."""
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((2 * i - 1) / (2.0 * i))
    return a


def power_seq(n, alpha):
    """
    Truncated coefficients of (1-z)^(-alpha).
    Valid for 0 < alpha < 2; robust for the small range used here.
    """
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((i + alpha - 1.0) / float(i))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    return a


def interpolate_sequence(seq, n):
    """Interpolate an existing sequence to length n and normalize."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.ndim != 1:
        seq = seq.ravel()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.maximum(seq, 0.0)
    if seq.size == n:
        p = seq.copy()
    else:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size, dtype=np.float64)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.interp(xn, xo, seq)
    return positive_normalize(p)


def get_provided_sequence():
    """Read height_sequence_1 from the global scope if present."""
    g = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if g is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(arr)) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr.ravel()


def make_reference_starts(n, rng, provider):
    """
    Build a list of normalized reference sequences for a fixed length n,
    ordered by local score.  No lambda functions are used.
    """
    raw_starts = []

    if provider is not None:
        raw_starts.append(interpolate_sequence(provider, n))

    raw_starts.append(central_binomial_seq(n))
    raw_starts.append(power_seq(n, 0.55))
    raw_starts.append(power_seq(n, 0.62))
    raw_starts.append(power_seq(n, 0.72))
    raw_starts.append(power_seq(n, 0.85))

    c = central_binomial_seq(n)
    if n > 1:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        t = np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)

    for beta in (-0.35, -0.20, 0.25, 0.55, 0.90):
        a = c * (1.0 + beta * t)
        if float(np.min(a)) > 0.0:
            raw_starts.append(a)

    # Smooth cap / arch
    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    raw_starts.append(np.sqrt(np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - x * x)) + 0.08)

    # U-shape endpoint emphasis
    if n > 1:
        raw_starts.append(1.0 + 0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)))
    else:
        raw_starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # A deterministic low-frequency waveform
    if n > 1:
        tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        tt = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    raw_starts.append(np.maximum(0.1, 1.0 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * tt + 0.7)))

    if rng is not None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(6):
            freq = rng.uniform(1.5, 8.0)
            amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.22)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * tt + phase)
        raw_starts.append(np.maximum(0.05, a))

    scored = []
    for a in raw_starts:
        p = positive_normalize(a)
        if p.size != n:
            continue
        sc = score_probs(p, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), p))

    if not scored:
        p0 = positive_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        return [p0]

    chosen = []
    remaining = scored
    max_refs = 4
    while len(chosen) < max_refs and remaining:
        best_i = 0
        for i in range(1, len(remaining)):
            if remaining[i][0] < remaining[best_i][0]:
                best_i = i
        chosen.append(remaining[best_i][1])
        del remaining[best_i]
    return chosen


def lag_derivative(p, n, k):
    """
    Centered unprojected derivative direction of convolution coefficient k
    with respect to probabilities p.

    This is d - p-dot(d), where d_i = 2 p_{k-i} for valid indices.
    """
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
    hi = min(k, n - 1)
    if hi >= lo:
        d[lo:hi + 1] = 2.0 * p[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
    m = float(np.dot(p, d))
    d -= m
    return d


def active_direction(p, c, K, tau_rel, rng=None):
    """
    Build a positive-space gradient direction from the top K convolution
    coefficients.  The top K are weighted by a softmax in their excess over
    the current maximum.  The returned vector is meant as a learning direction
    in log-probability space: theta -= lr * (active_direction / adaptive scale).
    """
    n = len(p)
    L = len(c)
    K = int(min(K, L - 1))
    if K < 1:
        K = 1

    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if K == 1:
        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        return lag_derivative(p, n, k)

    top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    tv = c[top]
    order = np.argsort(tv)[::-1]
    top = top[order]
    tv = tv[order]

    # Occasionally replace one coordinate to give a little stochastic breadth.
    if rng is not None and rng.random() < 0.08 and K < L // 2:
        j = int(rng.integers(0, K))
        top[j] = int(rng.integers(0, L))
        tv[j] = c[top[j]]

    tau = max(float(tau_rel) * maxc, 1e-300)
    z = (tv - maxc) / tau
    z = z - np.max(z)
    lam = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(lam))
    if s <= 0.0:
        lam = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / K
    else:
        lam = lam / s

    if float(np.max(lam)) > 0.999:
        best_lam_i = int(np.argmax(lam))
        k = int(top[best_lam_i])
        return lag_derivative(p, n, k)

    direction = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(K):
        w = float(lam[i])
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(top[i])
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi >= lo:
            direction[lo:hi + 1] += (2.0 * w) * p[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

    m = float(np.dot(p, direction))
    direction -= m

    if float(np.dot(direction, direction)) <= 1e-28:
        direction = lag_derivative(p, n, int(np.argmax(c)))
    return direction


def direction_schedule(frac, n):
    """Current active-set size and peak smoothing temperature."""
    if frac < 0.18:
        return max(8, int(round(0.11 * n))), 0.085
    if frac < 0.45:
        return max(4, int(round(0.06 * n))), 0.045
    if frac < 0.72:
        return max(3, int(round(0.025 * n))), 0.020
    if frac < 0.90:
        return max(1, int(round(0.008 * n))), 0.008
    return 1, 0.0025


def optimize_active(n, init_seq, refs, time_limit, rng, name="opt"):
    """
    Main local optimizer for a fixed length n.
    Returns (best_normalized_sequence, best_score).
    """
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        p0 = positive_normalize(init_seq)
        s0 = score_probs(p0, n)
        return p0, s0

    p = positive_normalize(init_seq)
    if p.size != n:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    theta = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
    theta = theta - np.max(theta)

    m_buf = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_buf = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bias1 = 1.0
    bias2 = 1.0
    step = 0

    c0 = self_convolution(p, n)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c0)))
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_theta = theta.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    last_improve = t0
    last_attempt = t0
    last_print = t0
    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    current_score = best_score

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = now - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        p = softmax_probs(theta)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if s <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            p = p / s

        c = self_convolution(p, n)
        current_score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))

        if current_score < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = current_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_theta = theta.copy()
            last_improve = now

        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(time_limit, 1e-12))
        K, tau_rel = direction_schedule(frac, n)
        direction = active_direction(p, c, K, tau_rel, rng)

        if now - max(last_improve, last_attempt) > 35.0:
            last_attempt = now
            accepted = False

            # 1) A deterministic line search in the current active direction.
            dnorm2 = float(np.dot(direction, direction))
            if dnorm2 > 1e-28:
                u = direction / np.sqrt(dnorm2)
                for hstep in (0.012, 0.03, 0.07, 0.14, 0.28):
                    th_try = theta - hstep * u
                    sc_try, p_try = score_of_theta(th_try, n)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_try
                        best_p = p_try.copy()
                        best_theta = th_try.copy()
                        theta = th_try.copy()
                        m_buf.fill(0.0)
                        v_buf.fill(0.0)
                        bias1 = 1.0
                        bias2 = 1.0
                        last_improve = time.monotonic()
                        accepted = True
                        break

            # 2) If line search failed, try a few reference/perturbation restarts.
            if not accepted:
                tried = 0
                while not accepted and tried < 4:
                    tried += 1
                    r = rng.random()
                    if r < 0.42:
                        scale = float(rng.choice((0.03, 0.07, 0.14, 0.28, 0.45)))
                        th_try = best_theta + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
                        p_try = softmax_probs(th_try)
                        p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 1e-300)
                        p_try = p_try / float(np.sum(p_try))
                    elif r < 0.78 and refs is not None and len(refs) > 0:
                        ref = refs[int(rng.integers(0, len(refs)))].copy()
                        mix = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.65))
                        p_try = (1.0 - mix) * best_p + mix * ref
                        p_try = positive_normalize(p_try)
                        th_try = np.log(np.maximum(p_try, 1e-12))
                        th_try = th_try - np.max(th_try)
                    else:
                        noise = exp_noise = 0.18 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                        p_try = best_p * np.exp(noise)
                        p_try = positive_normalize(p_try)
                        th_try = np.log(np.maximum(p_try, 1e-12))
                        th_try = th_try - np.max(th_try)

                    sc_try, p_chk = score_of_theta(th_try, n)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try):
                        if sc_try < best_score:
                            best_score = sc_try
                            best_p = p_chk.copy()
                            best_theta = th_try.copy()
                            theta = th_try.copy()
                            accepted = True
                        elif sc_try < best_score * 1.012 and rng.random() < 0.55:
                            theta = th_try.copy()
                            accepted = True

                    if accepted:
                        m_buf.fill(0.0)
                        v_buf.fill(0.0)
                        bias1 = 1.0
                        bias2 = 1.0
                        last_attempt = time.monotonic()
                if accepted:
                    continue

            # If we did not change the state, fall through and take one normal
            # Adam step instead of waiting idly.

        # Adam-like update in theta space using the positive-space direction.
        step += 1
        m_buf = beta1 * m_buf + (1.0 - beta1) * direction
        v_buf = beta2 * v_buf + (1.0 - beta2) * (direction * direction)
        bias1 *= beta1
        bias2 *= beta2

        m_hat = m_buf / (1.0 - bias1)
        v_hat = v_buf / (1.0 - bias2)

        lr = 0.18 * (1.0 - 0.75 * frac) + 0.010
        update = m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)
        theta = theta - lr * update
        theta = theta - np.max(theta)

        # Keep logits from escaping; this also keeps probabilities well above
        # underflow and maintains diversity.
        if float(np.min(theta)) < -30.0:
            np.maximum(theta, -30.0, out=theta)

        if now - last_print > 60.0:
            print(f"    {name}: t={elapsed:5.1f}s cur={current_score:.7f} best={best_score:.7f} K={K}")
            last_print = now

    p = softmax_probs(best_theta)
    p = normalize_probs(p, floor=1e-300)
    sc = score_probs(p, n)
    if sc < best_score:
        best_p = p
        best_score = sc
    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Required entry point.  Searches for the best valid sequence within the
    supplied time budget and returns it as a Python list of floats.
    """
    start_total = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_limit = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_limit = 2400.0
    total_limit = max(1.0, min(total_limit, 2400.0))
    reserve = 5.0 if total_limit < 120.0 else 40.0
    time_budget = max(2.0, total_limit - reserve)
    deadline = start_total + time_budget

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    provider = get_provided_sequence()

    # Choose the primary length.  The previously supplied construction is a
    # good default when its length is reasonable.
    n_primary = 384
    if provider is not None:
        m = int(provider.size)
        if 128 <= m <= 1536:
            n_primary = m
        elif m < 128:
            n_primary = 384
        else:
            n_primary = 1024

    best_score = np.inf
    best_p = None
    best_n = None

    def update_best(p, n, sc):
        nonlocal best_score, best_p, best_n
        if p is None or n is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.size != n:
            p = interpolate_sequence(p, n)
        p = positive_normalize(p)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_n = int(n)

    print("=" * 70)
    print("Peak-sculpting nonnegative step function search")
    print(f"Rough budget: {time_budget:.1f}s, primary n={n_primary}")
    print("=" * 70)

    refs_primary = make_reference_starts(n_primary, rng, provider)
    if refs_primary:
        sc0 = score_probs(refs_primary[0], n_primary)
        update_best(refs_primary[0], n_primary, sc0)
        print(f"Best initial: n={n_primary}, score={best_score:.7f}")

    def remaining():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    # ---------- Stage 1: primary-length primary start ----------
    if refs_primary and remaining() > 25.0:
        if time_budget > 700.0:
            t1 = min(0.55 * time_budget, remaining() - 350.0)
        else:
            t1 = 0.65 * remaining()
        t1 = max(0.0, t1)
        if t1 >= 10.0:
            print(f"\nStage 1: primary n={n_primary}, allocated {t1:.1f}s")
            p1, s1 = optimize_active(n_primary, refs_primary[0], refs_primary[1:], t1, rng, name="S1")
            update_best(p1, n_primary, s1)
            print(f"  Stage 1 best: n={best_n}, score={best_score:.7f}")

    # ---------- Stage 2: second start at primary length ----------
    if len(refs_primary) > 1 and best_score > 1.5027 and remaining() > 120.0:
        if time_budget > 700.0:
            t2 = min(0.20 * time_budget, remaining() - 180.0)
        else:
            t2 = 0.35 * remaining()
        t2 = max(0.0, t2)
        if t2 >= 15.0:
            print(f"\nStage 2: primary n={n_primary}, second reference, allocated {t2:.1f}s")
            p2, s2 = optimize_active(n_primary, refs_primary[1], refs_primary[:1] + refs_primary[2:], t2, rng, name="S2")
            update_best(p2, n_primary, s2)
            print(f"  Stage 2 best: n={best_n}, score={best_score:.7f}")

    # ---------- Stage 3: one secondary length if we are still above target ----------
    if best_score > 1.5027 and remaining() > 100.0:
        if best_n == 384:
            n_sec = 512
        elif best_n == 512:
            n_sec = 768
        elif best_n == 768:
            n_sec = 1024
        else:
            n_sec = 512

        init_sec = None
        if best_p is not None and best_n != n_sec:
            init_sec = interpolate_sequence(best_p, n_sec)
        refs_sec = make_reference_starts(n_sec, rng, provider)
        if init_sec is None and refs_sec:
            init_sec = refs_sec[0]
        if refs_sec:
            sc_sec_init = score_probs(refs_sec[0], n_sec)
            update_best(refs_sec[0], n_sec, sc_sec_init)

        if time_budget > 700.0:
            t3 = min(0.16 * time_budget, remaining() - 100.0)
        else:
            t3 = 0.30 * remaining()
        t3 = max(0.0, t3)
        if t3 >= 10.0 and init_sec is not None:
            print(f"\nStage 3: secondary n={n_sec}, allocated {t3:.1f}s")
            p3, s3 = optimize_active(n_sec, init_sec, refs_sec, t3, rng, name="S3")
            update_best(p3, n_sec, s3)
            print(f"  Stage 3 best: n={best_n}, score={best_score:.7f}")

    # ---------- Stage 4: final polish of current best ----------
    if best_p is not None and remaining() > 40.0:
        t4 = max(0.0, min(0.35 * time_budget, remaining() - 20.0))
        if t4 >= 10.0:
            # Rebuild references for the winning length.  If the winning length
            # was not primary, also use the current best in the reference list.
            refs_best = make_reference_starts(best_n, rng, provider)
            if best_p is not None:
                refs_best.insert(0, best_p.copy())

            # Include a mildly perturbed copy of the best sequence to break exact
            # stationarity during the polish.
            pert = best_p * np.exp(0.05 * rng.standard_normal(best_n))
            pert = positive_normalize(pert)
            if refs_best:
                refs_best.append(pert)

            print(f"\nStage 4: final polish n={best_n}, allocated {t4:.1f}s")
            p4, s4 = optimize_active(best_n, best_p, refs_best, t4, rng, name="S4")
            update_best(p4, best_n, s4)
            print(f"  Final polish best: n={best_n}, score={best_score:.7f}")

    # ---------- Output ----------
    if best_p is None or best_n is None:
        fallback_n = 384
        fallback = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64) * (100.0 / fallback_n)
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    # Scale to sum 100 for robust evaluation.  This is scale-invariant for the
    # score but keeps coefficients comfortably away from underflow/overflow.
    out = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64) * 100.0
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.max(out)) > 1000.0:
        out = out * (1000.0 / float(np.max(out)))
    if float(np.sum(out)) <= 0.01:
        out = np.ones(best_n, dtype=np.float64) * (100.0 / best_n)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in out]
    try:
        eval_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception as e:
        eval_score = best_score
        print("evaluate_sequence failed during final check:", repr(e))

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL n={len(final_list)}, local_best={best_score:.7f}, evaluated={eval_score:.7f}")
    print("=" * 70)

    return final_list
```
